## Adopted checked rows

Sources: research/20261011-m5-fix-rows.md; research/20261011-m5-settling-pass.md; research/20261011-m5-open-reads.md (CW/FK/TD/PL only). Unadopted last-reads not used.

>  M5-016: JsonTools::GetColorOptional (0x0084024C)
>
> **Paths:**
> - `!isMember(key)`: return false, silently (0x0084025C..0x00840262).
> - String value: `NamedColors::GetByString`, copy the 4 bytes, return true.
> - Array of size 3 or 4: r, g, b = `asFloat([0..2])`; a = `asFloat([3])` if size 4, else −1.0f.
>   - **If r > 1, g > 1 or b > 1** (`bhi`): the bytes are `vcvt.u32.f32` of the raw values. Alpha is written only when a >= 0 (the raw a). There is no log (0x008404D8..0x0084050C).
>   - **Otherwise** the components are converted one at a time, in the order r, g, b, then a if a >= 0: an optional warning, then `byte = vcvt.u32.f32(v*255.0f)` (truncating).
>   - With size 3 the alpha byte is left unchanged (`blt` 0x00840488).
> - Anything else: the warning below, return false.
>

## Call accounting and tests

See calls.md for the complete enclosing-function call census and explicit support/handoff boundary. Regression expectations are from the adopted rows; M5FixRowsTests and M3_013_CD12 verify widths, state/gates and structured log keys/levels/channels/text/arguments. Existing M5AnimationTests cover the unchanged source spans. No new emulator or hardware run. Final test counts/time are recorded in jobs/status/B-M5-1.md.
